1	using Microsoft.Xna.Framework;
2	using Microsoft.Xna.Framework.Graphics;
3	using ReLogic.Content;
4	using Terraria.DataStructures;
5	
6	namespace Terraria.Graphics.Renderers;
7	
8	public class FlameParticle : ABasicParticle
9	{
10		public float FadeOutNormalizedTime = 1f;
11	
12		private float _timeTolive;
13	
14		private float _timeSinceSpawn;
15	
16		private int _indexOfPlayerWhoSpawnedThis;
17	
18		private int _packedShaderIndex;
19	
20		public override void FetchFromPool()
21		{
22			base.FetchFromPool();
23			FadeOutNormalizedTime = 1f;
24			_timeTolive = 0f;
25			_timeSinceSpawn = 0f;
26			_indexOfPlayerWhoSpawnedThis = 0;
27			_packedShaderIndex = 0;
28		}
29	
30		public override void SetBasicInfo(Asset<Texture2D> textureAsset, Rectangle? frame, Vector2 initialVelocity, Vector2 initialLocalPosition)
31		{
32			base.SetBasicInfo(textureAsset, frame, initialVelocity, initialLocalPosition);
33			_origin = new Vector2(_frame.Width / 2, _frame.Height - 2);
34		}
35	
36		public void SetTypeInfo(float timeToLive, int indexOfPlayerWhoSpawnedIt, int packedShaderIndex)
37		{
38			_timeTolive = timeToLive;
39			_indexOfPlayerWhoSpawnedThis = indexOfPlayerWhoSpawnedIt;
40			_packedShaderIndex = packedShaderIndex;
41		}
42	
43		public override void Update(ref ParticleRendererSettings settings)
44		{
45			base.Update(ref settings);
46			_timeSinceSpawn += 1f;
47			if (_timeSinceSpawn >= _timeTolive)
48			{
49				base.ShouldBeRemovedFromRenderer = true;
50			}
51		}
52	
53		public override void Draw(ref ParticleRendererSettings settings, SpriteBatch spritebatch)
54		{
55			Color color = new Color(120, 120, 120, 60) * Utils.GetLerpValue(1f, FadeOutNormalizedTime, _timeSinceSpawn / _timeTolive, clamped: true);
56			Vector2 vector = settings.AnchorPosition + LocalPosition;
57			ulong seed = Main.TileFrameSeed ^ (((ulong)LocalPosition.X << 32) | (uint)LocalPosition.Y);
58			Player player = Main.player[_indexOfPlayerWhoSpawnedThis];
59			for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
60			{
61				DrawData drawData = new DrawData(position: vector + new Vector2(Utils.RandomInt(ref seed, -2, 3), Utils.RandomInt(ref seed, -2, 3)) * Scale, texture: _texture.Value, sourceRect: _frame, color: color, rotation: Rotation, origin: _origin, scale: Scale, effect: SpriteEffects.None);
62				drawData.shader = _packedShaderIndex;
63				DrawData cdd = drawData;
64				PlayerDrawHelper.SetShaderForData(player, 0, ref cdd);
65				cdd.Draw(spritebatch);
66			}
67			Main.pixelShader.CurrentTechnique.Passes[0].Apply();
68		}
69	}
70

```
using Microsoft.Xna.Framework;
using Microsoft.Xna.Framework.Graphics;
using ReLogic.Content;

namespace Terraria.Graphics.Renderers;

public abstract class ABasicParticle : IPooledParticle, IParticle
{
	public Vector2 AccelerationPerFrame;

	public Vector2 Velocity;

	public Vector2 LocalPosition;

	protected Asset<Texture2D> _texture;

	protected Rectangle _frame;

	protected Vector2 _origin;

	public float Rotation;

	public float RotationVelocity;

	public float RotationAcceleration;

	public Vector2 Scale;

	public Vector2 ScaleVelocity;

	public Vector2 ScaleAcceleration;

	public bool ShouldBeRemovedFromRenderer { get; protected set; }

	public bool IsRestingInPool { get; private set; }

	public ABasicParticle()
	{
		_texture = null;
		_frame = Rectangle.Empty;
		_origin = Vector2.Zero;
		Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
		LocalPosition = Vector2.Zero;
		ShouldBeRemovedFromRenderer = false;
	}

	public virtual void SetBasicInfo(Asset<Texture2D> textureAsset, Rectangle? frame, Vector2 initialVelocity, Vector2 initialLocalPosition)
	{
		_texture = textureAsset;
		_frame = (frame.HasValue ? frame.Value : _texture.Frame());
		_origin = _frame.Size() / 2f;
		Velocity = initialVelocity;
		LocalPosition = initialLocalPosition;
		ShouldBeRemovedFromRenderer = false;
	}

	public virtual void Update(ref ParticleRendererSettings settings)
	{
		Velocity += AccelerationPerFrame;
		LocalPosition += Velocity;
		RotationVelocity += RotationAcceleration;
		Rotation += RotationVelocity;
		ScaleVelocity += ScaleAcceleration;
		Scale += ScaleVelocity;
	}

	public abstract void Draw(ref ParticleRendererSettings settings, SpriteBatch spritebatch);

	public void RestInPool()
	{
		IsRestingInPool = true;
	}

	public virtual void FetchFromPool()
	{
		IsRestingInPool = false;
		ShouldBeRemovedFromRenderer = false;
		AccelerationPerFrame = Vector2.Zero;
		Velocity = Vector2.Zero;
		LocalPosition = Vector2.Zero;
		_texture = null;
		_frame = Rectangle.Empty;
		_origin = Vector2.Zero;
		Rotation = 0f;
		RotationVelocity = 0f;
		RotationAcceleration = 0f;
		Scale = Vector2.Zero;
		ScaleVelocity = Vector2.Zero;
		ScaleAcceleration = Vector2.Zero;
	}
}

```

	private static void Spawn_WallOfFleshGoatMountFlames(ParticleOrchestraSettings settings)
	{
		float num = 50f;
		for (int i = -1; i <= 1; i++)
		{
			int num2 = Main.rand.NextFromList(new short[3] { 326, 327, 328 });
			Main.instance.LoadProjectile(num2);
			Player player = Main.player[settings.IndexOfPlayerWhoInvokedThis];
			float num3 = Main.rand.NextFloat() * 0.9f + 0.1f;
			Vector2 vector = settings.PositionInWorld + new Vector2((float)i * 5.3333335f, 0f);
			FlameParticle flameParticle = _poolFlame.RequestParticle();
			flameParticle.SetBasicInfo(TextureAssets.Projectile[num2], null, Vector2.Zero, vector);
			flameParticle.SetTypeInfo(num, settings.IndexOfPlayerWhoInvokedThis, player.cMount);
			flameParticle.FadeOutNormalizedTime = 0.3f;
			flameParticle.ScaleAcceleration = Vector2.One * num3 * (-1f / 60f) / num;
			flameParticle.Scale = Vector2.One * num3;
			Main.ParticleSystem_World_BehindPlayers.Add(flameParticle);
			if (Main.rand.Next(8) == 0)
			{
				Dust dust = Dust.NewDustDirect(vector, 4, 4, 6, 0f, 0f, 100);
				if (Main.rand.Next(2) == 0)
				{
					dust.noGravity = true;
					dust.fadeIn = 1.15f;
				}
				else
				{
					dust.scale = 0.6f;
				}
				dust.velocity *= 0.6f;
				dust.velocity.Y -= 1.2f;
				dust.noLight = true;
				dust.position.Y -= 4f;
				dust.shader = GameShaders.Armor.GetSecondaryShader(player.cMount, player);
			}
		}
	}
}

```

```
Key details of the orchestration:
- num (timeToLive) = 50f
- loop i from -1 to 1 (3 particles)
- texture: random from [326, 327, 328] projectile textures
- num3 = rand * 0.9 + 0.1 → scale 0.1-1.0
- vector = settings.PositionInWorld + (i * 5.3333335, 0)
- SetBasicInfo(texture, null frame, Vector2.Zero velocity, vector local pos)
- SetTypeInfo(50, playerIndex, packedShaderIndex = player.cMount)
- FadeOutNormalizedTime = 0.3
- ScaleAcceleration = One * num3 * (-1/60) / 50
- Scale = One * num3
- dust segment 1/8 chance — already 1:1 in this repo

Note: Scale starts at num3 and ScaleAcceleration is num3 * (-1/60)/50 per frame^2 (scale velocity grows by this each frame). So scale shrinks quadratically. After 50 frames: scaleVelocity accumulated = 50 * num3 * (-1/60)/50 = -num3/60 per frame; total scale decrease = sum over t of t*a ≈ a * 50*51/2 = num3*(-1/60)/50 * 1275 = num3 * (-0.425). So the flame shrinks to about 0.575 * num3 scale at end of life. OK.

FadeOut: `Utils.GetLerpValue(1f, FadeOutNormalizedTime, _timeSinceSpawn / _timeTolive, clamped: true)` — note GetLerpValue(from=1, to=0.3, amount, clamped) which reverses: amount<=0.3 → 1, amount>=1 → 0. Actually GetLerpValue(a, b, c) computes (c-a)/(b-a). With a=1, b=0.3: at c=0.3 → (0.3-1)/(0.3-1) = 1; at c=1 → 0. So alpha fades linearly from full at 30% lifetime to 0 at end of life.

Color: new Color(120, 120, 120, 60) — i.e., RGB 120/120/120 with alpha 60 — multiplied by lerp value. So the flame is drawn dark gray semi-transparent (like a silhouette), with a shader applied (player.cMount armor shader = the dye of the mount... actually cMount is the "mount" shader index — armor shader for mount). Then 4 draws with random ±2 offsets jitter.

Note the Draw: draws 4 times each with random offset of (-2..2)*Scale — wait: `vector + new Vector2(RandomInt(-2,3), RandomInt(-2,3)) * Scale`. Utils.RandomInt(ref seed, -2, 3) — need to check inclusive/exclusive. In Terraria Utils.RandomInt(ref seed, min, max) is inclusive on both ends I believe: returns (int)(min + (seed % (ulong)(max - min + 1)))? Let me check. Actually let me check the Utils.RandomInt signature.

44	function frameOf(type: number): { fx: number; fy: number } {
45	  let fx = 10 * type, fy = 10 * (Math.random() * 3 | 0);

49	}

51	export class VanillaDust {
52	  private pool: VDust[] = [];
53	  private cursor = 0;

57	  constructor() {

61	        fadeIn: 0, noGravity: false, noLight: false, noLightEmittance: false,

69	  spawn(x: number, y: number, w: number, h: number, type: number,

71	    // 屏外裁剪需要视口信息——由 Game 侧 update 先暂存；spawn 时跳过（池小、
72	    // 视口裁剪在 update/draw 双重兜底），此处仅密度池满兜底
73	    let d: VDust | null = null;
74	    for (let i = 0; i < POOL; i++) {
75	      const c = this.pool[(this.cursor + i) % POOL];
76	      if (!c.active) { d = c; this.cursor = (this.cursor + i + 1) % POOL; break; }
77	    }
78	    if (!d) return null;
79	    const w2 = Math.max(5, w), h2 = Math.max(5, h);
80	    d.active = true;
81	    d.type = type;
82	    d.noGravity = false;
83	    d.color = color;
84	    d.alpha = alpha;
85	    d.x = x + (Math.random() * (w2 - 4) | 0) + 4;
86	    d.y = y + (Math.random() * (h2 - 4) | 0) + 4;
87	    d.vx = ((Math.random() * 41 | 0) - 20) * 0.1 + speedX;
88	    d.vy = ((Math.random() * 41 | 0) - 20) * 0.1 + speedY;
89	    const f = frameOf(type);
90	    d.fx = f.fx; d.fy = f.fy;
91	    d.rotation = 0;
92	    d.scale = (1 + ((Math.random() * 41 | 0) - 20) * 0.01) * scale;
93	    d.noLight = false;

95	    d.noLightEmittance = false;
96	    d.followPlayer = false;

100	      || type === 242 || type === 75 || type === 169 || type === 29 || (type >= 59 && type <= 65)
101	      || type === 158 || (type >= 293 && type <= 298) || type === 302 || type === 307 || type === 310) {
102	      d.vy = ((Math.random() * 16 | 0) - 10) * 0.1;
103	      d.vx *= 0.3;
104	      d.scale *= 0.7;
105	    }
106	    return d;
107	  }
108	
109	  /** UpdateDust（:423-2450，消费型精植）。playerDelta = 主角本 tick 位移
110	   *  （oldPosition 差——customData=Player 跟随用） */
111	  update(screenX: number, screenY: number, screenW: number, screenH: number,
112	    playerDx = 0, playerDy = 0): void {
113	    this.lights.length = 0;
114	    for (const d of this.pool) {
115	      if (!d.active) continue;
116	      // :445 scale>10 兜底
117	      if (d.scale > 10) { d.active = false; continue; }
118	      // :469 position += velocity
119	      d.x += d.vx; d.y += d.vy;
120	      // ---- 逐型段 ----
121	      switch (d.type) {

123	          if (!d.noLight) {
124	            this.lights.push({ x: d.x, y: d.y, r: (d.color?.r ?? 0) / 255 * d.scale * 0.4,
125	              g: (d.color?.g ?? 0) / 255 * d.scale * 0.4, b: (d.color?.b ?? 0) / 255 * d.scale * 0.4 });
126	          }
127	          if (d.followPlayer) {
128	            d.x += playerDx; d.y += playerDy;
129	            if (d.followOnce) d.followPlayer = false;
130	          }
131	          break;
132	        case 182:   // :685-705：rotation+1 + 光(1,0.2,0.1)×min(scale·0.25,1) + 一次性跟随
133	          d.rotation += 1;
134	          if (!d.noLight) {
135	            const k = Math.min(d.scale * 0.25, 1);
136	            this.lights.push({ x: d.x, y: d.y, r: k, g: k * 0.2, b: k * 0.1 });
137	          }
138	          if (d.followPlayer) {
139	            d.x += playerDx; d.y += playerDy;

141	          }
142	          break;
143	        case 261:   // :705-727：光(0.4,0.6,0.7)×min(scale·0.3,1) + 阻尼×2 档 + 持续跟随
144	          if (!d.noLight && !d.noLightEmittance) {
145	            const k = Math.min(d.scale * 0.3, 1);
146	            this.lights.push({ x: d.x, y: d.y, r: k * 0.4, g: k * 0.6, b: k * 0.7 });
147	          }
148	          if (d.noGravity) {
149	            d.vx *= 0.93; d.vy *= 0.93;
150	            if (d.fadeIn === 0) d.scale += 0.0025;
151	          }
152	          d.vx *= 0.97; d.vy *= 0.99;
153	          d.scale -= 0.0025;
154	          if (d.followPlayer) { d.x += playerDx; d.y += playerDy; }
155	          break;
156	        default:
157	          break;
158	      }
159	      // :1889-1897 重力（链头特型不在消费集）
160	      if (!d.noGravity && d.type !== 41 && d.type !== 44 && d.type !== 309) {
161	        d.vy += 0.1;
162	      }
163	      // :2144-2146 通用旋转
164	      if (d.type !== 79 && d.type !== 268 && d.type !== 304) {
165	        d.rotation += d.vx * 0.5;
166	      }
167	      // :2148-2171 fadeIn 生长目标语义
168	      if (d.fadeIn > 0 && d.fadeIn < 100) {
169	        if (d.type === 46 || d.type === 213 || d.type === 260) d.scale += 0.1;
170	        else if (d.type === 235) d.scale += 0.007;
171	        else d.scale += 0.03;
172	        if (d.scale > d.fadeIn) d.fadeIn = 0;
173	      } else if (d.type !== 304) {
174	        if (d.type === 213 || d.type === 260) d.scale -= 0.2;
175	        else d.scale -= 0.01;
176	      }

179	      // :2432-2436 缩没清除（dCount 五档密度缩放不建模——池小永不触发）
180	      if (d.scale < 0.1) d.active = false;

182	  }
183	
184	  /** 活动尘迭代（绘制用；fullbright=true 只取亮色族 {6,15,59-64}，false 取其余） */
185	  *each(fullbright: boolean): Generator<VDust> {
186	    for (const d of this.pool) {
187	      if (!d.active) continue;
188	      if (fullbrightnessWanted(fullbright, d)) yield d;
189	    }

192	  /** GetAlpha（:2469-2772 消费型精植）：返回乘数 [rgb, alpha]（0-1） */
193	  static alphaOf(d: VDust): { rgb: number; a: number } {
194	    const num = (255 - d.alpha) / 255;
195	    switch (d.type) {
196	      case 261: return { rgb: 230 / 255, a: 115 / 255 };                       // :2521
197	      case 6: return { rgb: 1, a: 25 / 255 };                                   // :2583
198	      case 182: return { rgb: 1, a: 0 };                                        // :2517
199	      case 264: {                                                               // :2508
200	        const c = d.color ?? { r: 0, g: 0, b: 0, a: 0 };
201	        return { rgb: 0.5, a: (clamp255(c.a / 8) / 255) * 0.5 };
202	      }
203	      case 86: case 87: case 88: case 89: case 90: case 91:
204	        if (!d.noLight) return { rgb: 1, a: 0 };                                // :2511
205	        return { rgb: num, a: num };
206	      default:
207	        return { rgb: num, a: num };
208	    }
209	  }
210	
211	  /** GetColor（:2773-2817 加性钳位）：second pass 色（尘自定义色与基色的叠加） */
212	  static colorPass(d: VDust, baseRgb: number, baseA: number): DustColor | null {
213	    if (!d.color) return null;
214	    return {
215	      r: clamp255(d.color.r - (255 - baseRgb * 255)) / 255,
216	      g: clamp255(d.color.g - (255 - baseRgb * 255)) / 255,
217	      b: clamp255(d.color.b - (255 - baseRgb * 255)) / 255,
218	      a: clamp255(d.color.a - (255 - baseA * 255)) / 255,
219	    };
220	  }
221	}
222	
223	function fullbrightnessWanted(wantFb: boolean, d: VDust): boolean {
224	  const fb = d.type === 6 || d.type === 15 || (d.type >= 59 && d.type <= 64);
225	  return wantFb === fb;
226	}

	public static int RandomInt(ref ulong seed, int max)
	{
		if ((max & -max) == max)

			num2 = num % max;
		}
		while (num - num2 + (max - 1) < 0);
		return num2;

<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

```
`RandomInt(ref seed, min, max)` = `RandomInt(ref seed, max - min) + min` → max-min is exclusive upper bound. So `RandomInt(seed, -2, 3)` → values in {-2,-1,0,1,2} (since RandomInt(seed, 5) returns 0..4). Good — 5 discrete values -2..2.

And the seed: `Main.TileFrameSeed ^ (((ulong)LocalPosition.X << 32) | (uint)LocalPosition.Y)` where LocalPosition is a Vector2 (floats) — casts to ulong/int. So the jitter is deterministic per position + TileFrameSeed (which changes over time). In canvas terms, a per-particle fixed seed derived from position XOR'd with a frame seed gives per-frame-changing jitter. I'll model this: seed = tileFrameSeed ^ ((int)x << 32 | (int)y) and implement RandomNext/RandomInt — does this repo have RandomNext? The repo has src/core/rng.ts with Crc32 etc. Let me check if there's a RandomNext implementation.

Actually simpler and equivalent in visual terms: 4 draws with jitter in {-2..2}×Scale using the repo RNG. But the task wants 1:1 spec: "初速零、scale 0.1-1.0、ScaleAcceleration（收缩）、FadeOutNormalizedTime 0.3、生命/帧率" and tests on "出生参数/收缩速率/淡出时间/存活期". A pure-random jitter is acceptable but let me be faithful and cheap: implement the seed-based jitter using a small LCG matching RandomNext. Let me check if repo has RandomNext in rng.ts.
```

77	/** RandomNextSeed :2388-2391：Java LCG (×25214903917 +11) & 2^48-1 */

82	function randomNext31(seedBox: { s: bigint }): number {
83	  seedBox.s = randomNextSeed(seedBox.s);

87	 *  否则拒绝采样（`num - num2 + (max-1) < 0` 的 Java int 溢出门）。 */
88	export function randomInt(seedBox: { s: bigint }, min: number, max: number): number {
89	  const range = max - min;
90	  if ((range & -range) === range) {
91	    return Number((BigInt(range) * BigInt(randomNext31(seedBox))) >> 31n) + min;
92	  }
93	  let bits: number, val: number;
94	  do {
95	    bits = randomNext31(seedBox);
96	    val = bits % range;
97	  } while (bits - val + (range - 1) < 0);
98	  return val + min;
99	}

394	  const discoTint = (): [number, number, number] => {
395	    const [r, g, b] = flicker!.discoColor();                            // :2738-2740 / :3466-3468
396	    const q = (v: number) => Math.round(v * 255 / 16) * 16;
397	    return [q(r), q(g), q(b)];
398	  };
399	  if (sheet === CAMPFIRE_FLAME.sheet) {
400	    const style = Math.floor(frameX / CAMPFIRE_FLAME.frameYPitch);   // :2736
401	    if (style === 5 && flicker) return discoTint();
402	    if (style === 14) return [50, 50, 100];                            // :2741-2743
403	    if (style === 15) return null;                                     // (255,255,255,200)
404	    return null;                                                       // (255,255,255,0)
405	  }
406	  if (sheet === 4) {
407	    const style = Math.floor(frameY / 22);                             // :3464
408	    if (style === 14 && flicker) return discoTint();
409	    if (style === 22) return [50, 50, 100];                            // :3469-3471
410	    if (style === 23) return null;                                     // (255,255,255,200)
411	    return [100, 100, 100];                                            // :3482 default
412	  }
413	  return [100, 100, 100];                                              // 其各族 default 同
414	}
415	
416	/** 火苗叠画主入口。调用点 = Renderer tile 层之后（原版 DrawSingleTile 内

418	 *  坐标 = 世界像素（调用方已在世界变换内）。 */

421	  tx0: number, ty0: number, tx1: number, ty1: number,
422	  flicker: FlickerClock | null,
423	): void {
424	  // 颜色/抖动只依赖 TileFrameSeed 与 FlickerClock（同原版，无时钟项）
425	  if (_drawCycle === 0) _frameSeed = randomNextSeed(_frameSeed);   // Main.cs:61156-61159
426	  _drawCycle = (_drawCycle + 1) % 5;

378	  it('constantJump 族 autoJump 语义（Player.cs:20366/:20406）：免松键连跳、不按跳不弹', () => {
379	    const w = mkWorld();
380	    const p = mkPlayer(w);
381	    tick(p, w, 8);
382	    p.mount.setMount(3, p);                       // constantJump = true（Mount.cs:848）
383	    expect(p.mount.autoJump).toBe(true);
384	    const groundY = p.y;
385	    // 不按跳仅移动：原版整条跳跃链在 if(controlJump) 门内（:20366），autoJump 只免
386	    // releaseJump（:20406）——绝不无输入自动弹跳（曾误实现为"移动即弹跳"）
387	    p.inputX = 1;
388	    let minY = p.y;
389	    for (let i = 0; i < 90; i++) { p.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, fakeHooks(w, p) as never); minY = Math.min(minY, p.y); }
390	    expect(minY).toBe(groundY);                   // 始终贴地
391	    // 按住跳不松手：autoJump 免 releaseJump 落地即重跳 → 持续弹跳
392	    p.inputX = 0; p.vx = 0; p.inputJump = true;
393	    minY = p.y;
394	    let landings = 0; let wasAir = false;
395	    for (let i = 0; i < 180; i++) {
396	      p.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, fakeHooks(w, p) as never);
397	      minY = Math.min(minY, p.y);
398	      if (!p.onGround) wasAir = true;
399	      else if (wasAir) { landings++; wasAir = false; }
400	    }
401	    expect(minY).toBeLessThan(groundY - 16);      // 离地腾空
402	    expect(landings).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2);   // 多次落地重跳（无需松键）
403	  });

610	  it('aimScutlix：±90° 外换向 + 眼部瞄准帧分档（6-10）', () => {

636	  it('SetMount 冷却下限 20（:6224）+ 冷却中充能 / 归零开火两连发 / 失瞄复位', () => {
637	    const m = new MountInstance();
638	    m.setMount(9, view());
639	    expect(m.abilityCooldown).toBe(20);          // 上坐即预热
640	    const target = { x: 300, y: 100, w: 20, h: 40 };
641	    const fired: number[][] = [];
642	    const v = view({ x: 100, y: 100 });
643	    // 冷却中：只瞄准 + 持续充能
644	    expect(m.updateScutlix(v, target, (x, y, vx, vy) => fired.push([x, y, vx, vy]))).toBe(false);
645	    expect(fired).toHaveLength(0);
646	    expect(m.abilityCharging).toBe(true);
647	    expect(m.aiming).toBe(true);
648	    // 冷却归零：两束眼激光 + 冷却重置 20
649	    m.abilityCooldown = 0;
650	    expect(m.updateScutlix(v, target, (x, y, vx, vy) => fired.push([x, y, vx, vy]))).toBe(true);
651	    expect(fired).toHaveLength(2);

653	    expect(m.abilityCharging).toBe(false);

656	      expect(x).toBeGreaterThan(100);               // 眼部出生点在玩家前方
657	      void y;
658	    }
659	    // 失瞄：停充 + 头部复位
660	    m.updateScutlix(v, null, () => { /* 无目标不开火 */ });
661	    expect(m.abilityCharging).toBe(false);
662	    expect(m.aiming).toBe(false);
663	    expect(m.frameExtra).toBe(0);
664	  });

743	describe('山羊 47 火焰编排器门（Mount.cs :3885-3896 SolidTileAllowBottomSlope）', () => {
744	  /** 落脚格视图桩：flags/type/half/slope/frameX 按需覆写（落脚格 = (tx,ty) 由调用方定） */
745	  const footStore = (tx: number, ty: number, over: {
746	    type?: number; half?: number; slope?: number; frameX?: number; liquid?: number; flagsOn?: boolean;
747	  } = {}) => {
748	    const w = 40, h = 40;
749	    const idx = (x: number, y: number) => y * w + x;
750	    const inBounds = (x: number, y: number) => x >= 0 && y >= 0 && x < w && y < h;
751	    const flags = new Uint8Array(w * h); flags[idx(tx, ty)] = over.flagsOn === false ? 0 : 1;
752	    const type = new Uint16Array(w * h); type[idx(tx, ty)] = over.type ?? 1;
753	    const half = new Uint8Array(w * h); half[idx(tx, ty)] = over.half ?? 0;
754	    const slope = new Uint8Array(w * h); slope[idx(tx, ty)] = over.slope ?? 0;
755	    const frameX = new Uint16Array(w * h); frameX[idx(tx, ty)] = over.frameX ?? 0;
756	    const liquid = new Uint8Array(w * h); liquid[idx(tx, ty)] = over.liquid ?? 0;
757	    return { w, h, inBounds, idx, flags, type, half, slope, frameX, liquid,
758	      isSolid: (x: number, y: number) => inBounds(x, y) && flags[idx(x, y)] !== 0,
759	    } as never;
760	  };
761	  const runGoat = (st: never) => {
762	    const m = new MountInstance();
763	    m.setMount(47, view());
764	    m.dashing = true;
765	    // 玩家底边落在 ty-1 → 落脚格 = ty（Mount.cs num25+1）
766	    const p = view({ y: 100, h: 42, vy: 0, store: st });
767	    const fires: Array<[number, number]> = [];
768	    m.updateMountEffects(p, {
769	      scutlixTargets: [], los: () => true, fishron: {
770	        hp: 100, maxHp: 100, inWater: false, inLava: false, inHoney: false,
771	        dripping: false, raining: false, windExposed: false,
772	      },
773	      goatFlames: (x, y) => fires.push([x, y]),
774	    });
775	    return fires;
776	  };
777	  const FOOT_Y = Math.trunc((100 + 42 - 1) / 16) + 1;   // (141)/16=8 → 落脚格 y=9
778	
779	  it('整砖实心无液 → 触发，出生点 = (tx×16+8, ty×16+16)', () => {
780	    const tx = Math.trunc((100 + 20 / 2) / 16);          // Center.X/16 = 6
781	    const fires = runGoat(footStore(tx, FOOT_Y));
782	    expect(fires).toHaveLength(1);
783	    expect(fires[0]).toEqual([tx * 16 + 8, FOOT_Y * 16 + 16]);   // ★纵向 +16 偏移（:3895）
784	  });
785	
786	  it('SolidTileAllowBottomSlope（cs:70172-70194）：平台放行 / 半砖拦 / 顶坡按帧 / 有液拦', () => {
787	    const tx = Math.trunc((100 + 20 / 2) / 16);
788	    // 平台（tileSolidTop）→ 放行
789	    const platId = TILE_DEFS.findIndex((d) => d.platform);
790	    expect(runGoat(footStore(tx, FOOT_Y, { type: platId }))).toHaveLength(1);
791	    // 半砖 → 拦
792	    expect(runGoat(footStore(tx, FOOT_Y, { half: 1 }))).toHaveLength(0);
793	    // 顶坡 slope 1 + 非平台 → 拦（topSlope=1|2，Tile.cs:320）
794	    expect(runGoat(footStore(tx, FOOT_Y, { slope: 1 }))).toHaveLength(0);
795	    expect(runGoat(footStore(tx, FOOT_Y, { slope: 2 }))).toHaveLength(0);
796	    // 顶坡 + 平台 + 合法顶帧（frameX 0 → 列 0 ∈[0,7]）→ 放行（cs:70223 豁免）
797	    expect(runGoat(footStore(tx, FOOT_Y, { type: platId, slope: 1, frameX: 0 }))).toHaveLength(1);
798	    // 顶坡 + 平台 + 非法帧（frameX 198 → 列 11 ∉ 合法段）→ 拦
799	    expect(runGoat(footStore(tx, FOOT_Y, { type: platId, slope: 1, frameX: 198 }))).toHaveLength(0);
800	    // 有液体 → 拦（:3891 tile.liquid == 0 门）
801	    expect(runGoat(footStore(tx, FOOT_Y, { liquid: 255 }))).toHaveLength(0);
802	    // 底坡（slope 3/4 非 topSlope）→ 放行
803	    expect(runGoat(footStore(tx, FOOT_Y, { slope: 3 }))).toHaveLength(1);
804	  });
805	
806	  it('非冲刺态/空中不触发（:3887 flag6 && velocity.Y==0）', () => {
807	    const tx = Math.trunc((100 + 20 / 2) / 16);
808	    const m = new MountInstance();
809	    m.setMount(47, view());
810	    m.dashing = false;
811	    const p = view({ y: 100, h: 42, vy: 0, store: footStore(tx, FOOT_Y) });
812	    let n = 0;
813	    m.updateMountEffects(p, {
814	      scutlixTargets: [], los: () => true, fishron: {
815	        hp: 100, maxHp: 100, inWater: false, inLava: false, inHoney: false,
816	        dripping: false, raining: false, windExposed: false,
817	      },
818	      goatFlames: () => { n++; },
819	    });
820	    expect(n).toBe(0);
821	  });
822	});
823	
824	describe('猪鲨崽 12：MountFishronSpecial 湿身语义（Player.cs:3882 + Mount.cs:3354/:4996）', () => {
825	  const fv = (over: Partial<Parameters<MountInstance['fishronSpecial']>[0]> = {}) => ({
826	    hp: 100, maxHp: 100, inWater: false, inLava: false, inHoney: false,
827	    dripping: false, raining: false, windExposed: false, ...over,
828	  });
829	
830	  it('增益真值表：湿身/半血以下/雨中露天 = 全额；干爽满血无雨 = 减半', () => {
831	    const m = new MountInstance();
832	    m.setMount(12, view());
833	    expect(m.fishronSpecial(fv())).toBe(false);               // 干爽满血无雨
834	    expect(m.fishronSpecial(fv({ inWater: true }))).toBe(true);   // ★湿身是全额（旧实现误减半）
835	    expect(m.fishronSpecial(fv({ inLava: true }))).toBe(false);   // 岩浆湿不算
836	    expect(m.fishronSpecial(fv({ inHoney: true }))).toBe(false);  // 蜂蜜湿不算
837	    expect(m.fishronSpecial(fv({ hp: 40 }))).toBe(true);      // 半血以下
838	    expect(m.fishronSpecial(fv({ raining: true, windExposed: true }))).toBe(true);
839	    expect(m.fishronSpecial(fv({ raining: true, windExposed: false }))).toBe(false);
840	  });

842	  it('计数器维护（:4996-5003：半血→60 / 湿身或雨中露天→420）', () => {
843	    const m = new MountInstance();
844	    m.setMount(12, view());
845	    m.updateFishron(fv({ inWater: true }));
846	    expect(m.fishronCounter).toBe(420);
847	    expect(m.fishronBoost).toBe(true);
848	    m.updateFishron(fv({ hp: 40 }));
849	    expect(m.fishronCounter).toBe(60);
850	    // 计数器 >0 期间即便已干爽也保持增益（出水后 420t 余效）
851	    m.updateFishron(fv());
852	    expect(m.fishronBoost).toBe(true);
853	    expect(m.fishronCounter).toBe(59);                        // 先衰减再判（:25340）
854	    m.fishronCounter = 0;
855	    expect(m.fishronSpecial(fv())).toBe(false);
856	  });
857	
858	  it('dripping 消费（Player.cs:3886 `!dripping`）：buff 103 Wet 激活 → 全额增益', () => {
859	    const m = new MountInstance();
860	    m.setMount(12, view());
861	    // 干爽满血无雨无 Wet → 减半
862	    expect(m.fishronSpecial(fv({ dripping: true }))).toBe(true);
863	    // dripping 只进增益判定——updateFishron 不因 Wet 写计数器（Mount.cs :4996-5003
864	    // 计数器仅由 wet | 雨中露天驱动）
865	    m.updateFishron(fv({ dripping: true, inWater: false, raining: false }));
866	    expect(m.fishronCounter).toBe(0);
867	    expect(m.fishronBoost).toBe(true);
868	    // Wet 到期（dripping 回落）且无其他增益源 → 减半恢复

872	  it('liquidAlpha 渐变因子（Mount.Draw case 12 :5780-5787）：clamp(计数器/60, 0, 1)', () => {
873	    // num8 = clamp(MountFishronSpecialCounter/60f, 0f, 1f)；color.A=127 后整色 × num8
874	    expect(fishronWashFactor(0)).toBe(0);
875	    expect(fishronWashFactor(30)).toBeCloseTo(0.5);

1180	  it('MountInstance.updateBodyRotation：fullRotation 写入 + 钻头外环随 vx/80 累积（:3499）', () => {
1181	    const m = new MountInstance();
1182	    m.setMount(8, view());
1183	    const v = view({ vx: 4, vy: 0 });
1184	    m.updateBodyRotation(v);
1185	    expect(m.fullRotation).toBeCloseTo(Math.PI / 4 * 0.95 / 2);   // 4/4=1 → 钳 0.95

43	  // ---- 天气环境音循环轨（SceneState.cs:235-297 暴风雪双轨；SoundType.Ambient）----
44	  | 'blizzardStrong'    // blizzard_strong_loop（SoundID.cs:821 BlizzardStrongLoop，WithVolume(0.5)）
45	  | 'blizzardInside'    // blizzard_inside_building_loop（:819 BlizzardInsideBuildingLoop）
46	  | 'sandstormWind';    // ★登记缺失：原版沙暴无专属 wav（Sandstorm 只有视觉 shader + 风场 BGM），
47	                        //   全量 852 wav 中无 sandstorm/wind 条目——素材出现前 playLoop 恒 null
48	
49	/** 天气环境音循环轨名（Weather.update 状态机 ↔ Sfx 循环句柄的接口面） */
50	export type WeatherLoopName = 'blizzardStrong' | 'blizzardInside' | 'sandstormWind';
51	
52	/** 循环轨基础音量（SoundID 定义侧的 WithVolume，SfxName 注释同源） */
53	export const LOOP_BASE_VOL: Record<WeatherLoopName, number> = {
54	  blizzardStrong: 0.5,    // SoundID.cs:821 WithVolume(0.5f)
55	  blizzardInside: 1,      // :819 无 WithVolume → 1.0
56	  sandstormWind: 1,       // 素材缺失，占位
57	};
58	
59	/** 循环音句柄（SceneState.cs:235-297 暴风雪双轨的常驻轨管理面：
60	 *  原版 PlayTrackedSound 拿 SlotId 后每帧改 Volume，轨不拆；此处同构） */
61	export interface SfxLoop {
62	  /** 停止并释放节点（幂等） */
63	  stop(): void;
64	  /** 目标音量 0-1（setTargetAtTime 平滑逼近 ~0.2s，防切换爆音） */
65	  setVolume(v: number): void;
66	  /** 节点是否仍在发声（含缓冲解码完成后起振；pending 期为 false） */
67	  readonly playing: boolean;
68	  /** 终态（stop() 或缓冲加载失败）——调用方可丢弃重建；
69	   *  非 dead 且 !playing = 解码在途，勿重建（否则每帧泄漏 GainNode） */
70	  readonly dead: boolean;
71	}

181	
182	export class Sfx {
183	  private ctx: AudioContext | null = null;
184	  private buffers = new Map<string, AudioBuffer>();
185	  /** 在途解码中的文件（防重复 fetch） */
186	  private pending = new Set<string>();
187	  /** 循环轨在途解码（Promise 去重，与 pending 互斥使用） */
188	  private bufferPromises = new Map<string, Promise<AudioBuffer | null>>();
189	  /** 取回失败/不存在的文件（负缓存，防 404 每次重试） */
190	  private failed = new Set<string>();
191	  muted = false;
192	  /** 主音量 0..1（M6 设置页） */
193	  master = 1;
194	  /** 载入期静音门（Game 侧置位/解除）：afterWorldLoad 的 waterCheck 全图收敛期间，
195	   *  killTile/breakTile/合并音会以 listener=(0,0) 满响（"进世界音效爆发"根因）。
196	   *  只盖一次性播放入口（play/playWavFile/startTrackedLoop——playWav/playFiles
197	   *  漏斗进 playWavFile 已被盖）；循环轨（天气/BGM/雨声）不经此门 */
198	  suppress = false;
199	
200	  /** 按需取缓冲：未命中时后台 fetch+decode，同步返回已就位缓冲或 null */
201	  private ensureBuffer(file: string): AudioBuffer | null {
202	    const buf = this.buffers.get(file);
203	    if (buf) return buf;
204	    if (this.pending.has(file) || this.failed.has(file)) return null;
205	    const ctx = this.ensure(true);
206	    if (!ctx) return null;
207	    this.pending.add(file);
208	    fetch(`sounds/${file}.wav`)
209	      .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.arrayBuffer() : null))
210	      .then((ab) => (ab ? ctx.decodeAudioData(ab) : null))
211	      .then((decoded) => {
212	        if (decoded) this.buffers.set(file, decoded);
213	        else this.failed.add(file);
214	      })
215	      .catch(() => { this.failed.add(file); })
216	      .finally(() => { this.pending.delete(file); });
217	    return null;
218	  }
219	
220	  /** 定向预热（不阻塞）：把指定 wav 后台取齐，播放时即命中 */
221	  preloadFiles(files: Iterable<string>) {
222	    for (const f of files) this.ensureBuffer(f);
223	  }
224	
225	  /** 按逻辑名预热（WAV_MAP 展开到具体文件） */
226	  preloadNames(names: SfxName[]) {
227	    for (const n of names) for (const f of WAV_MAP[n] ?? []) this.ensureBuffer(f);
228	  }
229	
230	  // ---- 雨声层：原版 RainAmbience（Music_28.mp3，XACT Wave Bank 同源提取）循环 ----
231	  private rainGain: GainNode | null = null;
232	  private rainSrc: AudioBufferSourceNode | null = null;
233	  private rainLoaded = false;
234	  /** 设置雨声强度 0-1（每帧调用；0 时静音不拆节点——重启有冷启动开销） */
235	  setRain(vol: number) {
236	    const ctx = this.ensure(true);
237	    if (!ctx) return;
238	    if (!this.rainGain) {
239	      const g = ctx.createGain();
240	      g.gain.value = 0;
241	      g.connect(ctx.destination);
242	      this.rainGain = g;
243	      // 原版雨环境音轨（RainAmbience）懒加载循环；加载失败静默（雨声可选）
244	      fetch('audios/music/Music_28.mp3')
245	        .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.arrayBuffer() : null))
246	        .then((ab) => (ab ? ctx.decodeAudioData(ab) : null))
247	        .then((buf) => {
248	          if (!buf) return;
249	          const src = ctx.createBufferSource();
250	          src.buffer = buf;
251	          src.loop = true;
252	          src.connect(this.rainGain!);
253	          src.start();
254	          this.rainSrc = src;
255	          this.rainLoaded = true;
256	        })
257	        .catch(() => {});
258	    }
259	    const v = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, vol)) * this.master * 0.6;
260	    this.rainGain.gain.setTargetAtTime(v, ctx.currentTime, 0.4);
261	  }
262	
263	  /** 世界声距离衰减（LegacySoundPlayer.cs:160 SoundAttenuationDistance = 2500px） */
264	  static readonly ATTEN = 2500;
265	  /** 监听器（原版 Main.Camera.Center，相机中心非玩家）；Game 每帧更新。未进世界=(0,0) */
266	  private lx = 0; private ly = 0;
267	  setListener(x: number, y: number) { this.lx = x; this.ly = y; }
268	  /** 原版距离衰减（LegacySoundPlayer.cs:381-401）：dist ≥2500 → 不播；
269	   *  vol × (1 - dist/2500)。不传坐标（x=-1 语义）→ 全量（UI/玩家自身动作声） */
270	  private atten(vol: number, x?: number, y?: number): number {
271	    if (x == null || y == null) return vol;
272	    const d = Math.hypot(x - this.lx, y - this.ly);
273	    if (d >= Sfx.ATTEN) return 0;
274	    return vol * (1 - d / Sfx.ATTEN);
275	  }
276	
277	  private ensure(silent = false): AudioContext | null {
278	    if (this.muted && !silent) return null;
279	    if (!this.ctx) {
280	      const AC = window.AudioContext ?? (window as unknown as { webkitAudioContext: typeof AudioContext }).webkitAudioContext;
281	      if (!AC) return null;
282	      this.ctx = new AC();
283	    }
284	    if (this.ctx.state === 'suspended') this.ctx.resume().catch(() => {});
285	    return this.ctx;
286	  }
287	
288	  /** 播放原版 wav（随机变体 + 轻微音高扰动）；vol 0-1 */
289	  playWav(name: SfxName, vol = 1, x?: number, y?: number): boolean {
290	    const list = WAV_MAP[name];
291	    if (!list || !list.length) return false;
292	    return this.playWavFile(list[Math.floor(Math.random() * list.length)], vol, x, y);
293	  }
294	
295	  /** 播放任意 wav 文件列表（随机选 + 音高扰动）；供怪物专属声效用 */
296	  playFiles(files: string[], vol = 1, x?: number, y?: number): boolean {
297	    if (!files.length) return false;
298	    return this.playWavFile(files[Math.floor(Math.random() * files.length)], vol, x, y);
299	  }
300	
301	  /** 跟踪式循环音（SoundEngine.PlayTrackedLoopedSound 语义，AI_016 :44276 引信嘶声）:
302	   *  返回句柄 {stop, setPos}——setPos 按距离衰减刷新音量（ProjectileAudioTracker
303	   *  跟随弹体）。缓冲未就绪返回 null（调用方下 tick 重试即可）。 */
304	  startTrackedLoop(name: SfxName, vol: number, x: number, y: number): { stop(): void; setPos(px: number, py: number): void } | null {
305	    if (this.suppress) return null;          // 载入期静音门（见字段注释）
306	    const list = WAV_MAP[name];
307	    if (!list || !list.length) return null;
308	    const ctx = this.ensure();
309	    if (!ctx) return null;
310	    const buf = this.ensureBuffer(list[0]);
311	    if (!buf) return null;
312	    const src = ctx.createBufferSource();
313	    src.buffer = buf;
314	    src.loop = true;
315	    src.playbackRate.value = 0.92 + Math.random() * 0.16;
316	    const g = ctx.createGain();
317	    g.gain.value = this.atten(vol, x, y) * this.master;
318	    src.connect(g).connect(ctx.destination);
319	    src.start();
320	    return {
321	      stop: () => { try { src.stop(); } catch { /* 已停 */ } g.disconnect(); },
322	      setPos: (px: number, py: number) => {
323	        g.gain.value = this.atten(vol, px, py) * this.master;
324	      },
325	    };
326	  }
327	
328	  // ---- 环境循环轨（SoundEngine.PlayTrackedSound 语义：常驻节点 + 音量平滑跟随）----
329	
330	  /** 异步取缓冲（ensureBuffer 的 Promise 版，供循环轨"缓冲就绪后再起振"） */
331	  private ensureBufferAsync(file: string, ctx: AudioContext): Promise<AudioBuffer | null> {
332	    const hit = this.buffers.get(file);
333	    if (hit) return Promise.resolve(hit);
334	    if (this.failed.has(file)) return Promise.resolve(null);
335	    const pending = this.bufferPromises.get(file);
336	    if (pending) return pending;
337	    const p = fetch(`sounds/${file}.wav`)
338	      .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.arrayBuffer() : null))
339	      .then((ab) => (ab ? ctx.decodeAudioData(ab) : null))
340	      .then((decoded) => {
341	        if (decoded) { this.buffers.set(file, decoded); return decoded; }
342	        this.failed.add(file);
343	        return null;
344	      })
345	      .catch(() => { this.failed.add(file); return null; })
346	      .finally(() => { this.bufferPromises.delete(file); });
347	    this.bufferPromises.set(file, p);
348	    return p;
349	  }
350	
351	  /** 循环轨底层（任意 wav 文件 + 外部基础音量）；缓冲未就位时句柄先行返回，
352	   *  解码完成后自动起振——调用方无需重试 */
353	  private startLoopFile(file: string, vol: number, base: number): SfxLoop | null {
354	    const ctx = this.ensure();
355	    if (!ctx) return null;
356	    const gain = ctx.createGain();
357	    gain.gain.value = 0;
358	    gain.connect(ctx.destination);
359	    const st = {
360	      src: null as AudioBufferSourceNode | null,
361	      vol: Math.max(0, Math.min(1, vol)),
362	      base,
363	      started: false,
364	      stopped: false,
365	      failed: false,
366	    };
367	    const apply = () => {
368	      const target = Math.min(1, st.vol * st.base * this.master);
369	      gain.gain.setTargetAtTime(target, ctx.currentTime, 0.2);
370	    };
371	    const handle: SfxLoop = {
372	      stop: () => {
373	        if (st.stopped) return;
374	        st.stopped = true;
375	        try { st.src?.stop(); } catch { /* 已停 */ }
376	        st.src?.disconnect();
377	        gain.disconnect();
378	      },
379	      setVolume: (v: number) => {
380	        st.vol = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, v));
381	        if (st.started && !st.stopped) apply();
382	      },
383	      get playing() { return st.started && !st.stopped; },
384	      get dead() { return st.stopped || st.failed; },
385	    };
386	    void this.ensureBufferAsync(file, ctx).then((buf) => {
387	      if (st.stopped) return;
388	      if (!buf) { st.failed = true; gain.disconnect(); return; }
389	      const src = ctx.createBufferSource();
390	      src.buffer = buf;
391	      src.loop = true;
392	      src.connect(gain);
393	      src.start();
394	      st.src = src;
395	      st.started = true;
396	      apply();
397	    });
398	    return handle;
399	  }
400	
401	  /**
402	   * 环境循环轨播放（SoundEngine.PlayTrackedSound 语义）：返回常驻句柄，
403	   * 音量经 setVolume 每帧跟随（天气状态机驱动）。默认音量 0.3（不吵）。
404	   * 无素材轨（如 sandstormWind）返回 null——调用方跳过即可，无需重试。
405	   */
406	  playLoop(name: WeatherLoopName, vol = 0.3): SfxLoop | null {
407	    const list = WAV_MAP[name];
408	    if (!list || !list.length) return null;
409	    return this.startLoopFile(list[0], vol, LOOP_BASE_VOL[name]);
410	  }
411	
412	  /** 按文件名播放循环（沙暴等未来素材接入的备用口） */
413	  playLoopFile(file: string, vol = 0.3, base = 1): SfxLoop | null {
414	    return this.startLoopFile(file, vol, base);
415	  }
416	
417	  /** 按文件名直播（缓冲未就位时触发后台加载，本播返回 false 走合成兜底）。
418	   *  vol 语义 = 原版 volumeScale（调用方传 1 = 原版默认）；原版各 case 的专属
419	   *  音量修正（FILE_CASE_VOL）在此单点统一乘，调用方无需手传修正系数 */
420	  playWavFile(file: string, vol = 1, x?: number, y?: number): boolean {
421	    if (this.suppress) return false;         // 载入期静音门（见字段注释）
422	    const ctx = this.ensure();
423	    if (!ctx) return false;
424	    const v = this.atten(vol, x, y);
425	    if (v <= 0) return false;
426	    vol = v * (FILE_CASE_VOL[file] ?? 1);
427	    const buf = this.ensureBuffer(file);
428	    if (!buf) return false;
429	    const src = ctx.createBufferSource();
430	    src.buffer = buf;
431	    src.playbackRate.value = 0.92 + Math.random() * 0.16;
432	    const g = ctx.createGain();
433	    g.gain.value = Math.min(1, vol * this.master);
434	    src.connect(g).connect(ctx.destination);
435	    src.start();
436	    return true;
437	  }
438	
439	  play(name: SfxName, vol = 1, x?: number, y?: number) {
440	    if (this.suppress) return;               // 载入期静音门（见字段注释）
441	    // 优先原版素材，失败回退合成（master 在 playWavFile/osc 各自生效一次）。
442	    // 带坐标时衰减（atten），≤0 直接不播——合成回退也不触发
443	    if (x != null && y != null && this.atten(vol, x, y) <= 0) return;
444	    if (this.playWav(name, vol, x, y)) return;
445	    const ctx = this.ensure();
446	    if (!ctx) return;
447	    const t = ctx.currentTime;
448	    const osc = (type: OscillatorType, f0: number, f1: number, dur: number, vol: number, delay = 0) => {
449	      const o = ctx.createOscillator();
450	      const g = ctx.createGain();
451	      o.type = type;
452	      o.frequency.setValueAtTime(f0, t + delay);
453	      o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(1, f1), t + delay + dur);
454	      g.gain.setValueAtTime(vol * this.master, t + delay);
455	      g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, t + delay + dur);
456	      o.connect(g).connect(ctx.destination);
457	      o.start(t + delay);
458	      o.stop(t + delay + dur);
459	    };
460	    switch (name) {
461	      case 'hit': osc('square', 320, 110, 0.09, 0.12); break;
462	      case 'chop': osc('triangle', 220, 80, 0.12, 0.16); break;
463	      case 'hurt': osc('sawtooth', 180, 60, 0.22, 0.14); break;
464	      case 'killed': osc('square', 260, 60, 0.25, 0.14); break;
465	      case 'pkilled': osc('sawtooth', 160, 40, 0.6, 0.18); break;
466	      case 'zombie': osc('sawtooth', 90, 55, 0.5, 0.1); break;
467	      case 'roar': osc('sawtooth', 70, 40, 0.9, 0.2); break;
468	      case 'pickup': osc('sine', 520, 990, 0.09, 0.1); osc('sine', 780, 1320, 0.08, 0.06, 0.05); break;
469	      case 'dig': osc('square', 140, 90, 0.05, 0.06); break;
470	      case 'tink': osc('sine', 900, 500, 0.06, 0.08); break;
471	      case 'place': osc('triangle', 260, 120, 0.08, 0.12); break;
472	      case 'drink': osc('sine', 300, 620, 0.06, 0.08); osc('sine', 420, 780, 0.06, 0.07, 0.07); osc('sine', 560, 940, 0.06, 0.06, 0.14); break;
473	      case 'splash': osc('sine', 700, 200, 0.18, 0.1); break;
474	      case 'summon': {
475	        // 召唤杖 UseSound(Item_44)近似:短促"噗"——高频下扫正弦 + 一撮噪声气声
476	        osc('sine', 900, 260, 0.16, 0.14);
477	        osc('triangle', 1400, 500, 0.08, 0.06);
478	        break;
479	      }
480	      case 'whipCrack': {
481	        // 鞭响(Item_152)近似:高频噪声急促炸开 + 快速衰减
482	        const wDur = 0.12, wn = Math.floor(ctx.sampleRate * wDur);
483	        const wBuf = ctx.createBuffer(1, wn, ctx.sampleRate);
484	        const wd = wBuf.getChannelData(0);
485	        for (let i = 0; i < wn; i++) wd[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * Math.pow(1 - i / wn, 3);
486	        const wSrc = ctx.createBufferSource();
487	        wSrc.buffer = wBuf;
488	        const hp = ctx.createBiquadFilter();
489	        hp.type = 'highpass';
490	        hp.frequency.value = 1200;
491	        const wg = ctx.createGain();
492	        wg.gain.value = 0.18 * this.master;
493	        wSrc.connect(hp).connect(wg).connect(ctx.destination);
494	        wSrc.start(t);
495	        break;
496	      }
497	      case 'explosion': {
498	        // 噪声爆发近似(原版 Item_14):一次性白噪 buffer + 低频 thump。
499	        // ★曾无此分支:按需加载首播(wav 还在 fetch)完全静音 = "炸弹没有音效"主因
500	        const dur = 0.5, n = Math.floor(ctx.sampleRate * dur);
501	        const buf = ctx.createBuffer(1, n, ctx.sampleRate);
502	        const d = buf.getChannelData(0);
503	        for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) d[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * Math.pow(1 - i / n, 2.2);
504	        const src = ctx.createBufferSource();
505	        src.buffer = buf;
506	        const lp = ctx.createBiquadFilter();
507	        lp.type = 'lowpass';
508	        lp.frequency.setValueAtTime(900, t);
509	        lp.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(120, t + dur);
510	        const g = ctx.createGain();
511	        g.gain.value = 0.5 * this.master;
512	        src.connect(lp).connect(g).connect(ctx.destination);
513	        src.start(t);
514	        osc('sine', 90, 35, 0.35, 0.35); // 低频冲击
515	        break;
516	      }
517	      case 'coin': osc('sine', 990, 1400, 0.07, 0.08); osc('sine', 1320, 1760, 0.09, 0.06, 0.05); break;
518	      case 'door_open': osc('triangle', 180, 320, 0.12, 0.1); break;
519	      case 'door_close': osc('triangle', 320, 160, 0.1, 0.1); break;
520	      case 'shatter': osc('square', 1400, 300, 0.15, 0.1); break;
521	      case 'drown': osc('sine', 400, 90, 0.4, 0.1); break;
522	      case 'mirror': osc('sine', 700, 1900, 0.3, 0.06); osc('sine', 1400, 2800, 0.25, 0.04, 0.1); break;   // 魔镜闪烁上行
523	      case 'conch': osc('sine', 220, 330, 0.25, 0.08); osc('sine', 330, 440, 0.3, 0.06, 0.2); break;        // 海螺低鸣
524	    }
525	  }
526	}

import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';

import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { TILE_DEFS } from '../src/data/tiles';
import vanillaJson from '../public/sprites/vanilla.json';
// @ts-expect-error mjs 无类型声明(豁免表同源锁:与 tools/atlas-lint.mjs 保持一致)

describe('atlas-lint', () => {
  it('CLI:0 error(构建期阻断基线)', () => {

  });

    const tiles = (vanillaJson as { tiles: Record<string, unknown> }).tiles;

    }
  });

        bad.push(`${d.key} ${w}×${h} vs ${fs0[0]}×${fs0[1]}`);
      }
    }
    expect(bad).toEqual([]);
  });

    const fs = require('node:fs');

      return { w: b.readUInt32BE(16), h: b.readUInt32BE(20) };

```
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace Terraria.Graphics.Renderers;

public class ParticlePool<T> where T : IPooledParticle
{
	public delegate T ParticleInstantiator();

	private ParticleInstantiator _instantiator;

	private List<T> _particles;

	public int CountParticlesInUse()
	{
		int num = 0;
		for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
		{
			if (!_particles[i].IsRestingInPool)
			{
				num++;
			}
		}
		return num;
	}

	public ParticlePool(int initialPoolSize, ParticleInstantiator instantiator)
	{
		_particles = new List<T>(initialPoolSize);
		_instantiator = instantiator;
	}

	public T RequestParticle()
	{
		if (Main.NoPooling)
		{
			_particles.RemoveAll((T p) => p.IsRestingInPool);
		}
		int count = _particles.Count;
		for (int num = 0; num < count; num++)
		{
			if (_particles[num].IsRestingInPool)
			{
				_particles[num].FetchFromPool();
				return _particles[num];
			}
		}
		T val = _instantiator();
		_particles.Add(val);
		val.FetchFromPool();
		return val;
	}
}

```

```
public static float GetLerpValue(float from, float to, float t, bool clamped = false)
{
    if (clamped)
    {
        if (from < to) { if (t < from) return 0; if (t > to) return 1; }
        else { if (t < to) return 1; if (t > from) return 0; }
    }
    return (t - from) / (to - from);
}
```

```
	public static float GetLerpValue(float from, float to, float t, bool clamped = false)
	{
		if (clamped)
		{
			if (from < to)
			{
				if (t < from)
				{
					return 0f;
				}
				if (t > to)
				{
					return 1f;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if (t < to)
				{
					return 1f;
				}
				if (t > from)
				{
					return 0f;
				}
			}
		}
		return (t - from) / (to - from);
	}

	public static float Remap(float fromValue, float fromMin, float fromMax, float toMin, float toMax, bool clamped = true)
	{
		return MathHelper.Lerp(toMin, toMax, GetLerpValue(fromMin, fromMax, fromValue, clamped));
	}

	public static double Remap(double fromValue, double fromMin, double fromMax, double toMin, double toMax, bool clamped = true)
	{
		return Lerp(toMin, toMax, GetLerpValue(fromMin, fromMax, fromValue, clamped));
	}

	public static double EaseOutBounce(double x)
	{
		return BounceEaseOut(x, 4, 2.0);
	}

	{

to) return 1;\n  } else {\n    if (t < to) return 1;\n    if (t > from) return 0;\n  }\n  return (t - from) / (to - from);\n}\n\n/** 淡出系数（生命归一比 t01 = _timeSinceSpawn / timeToLive）：前 30% 全亮，\n *  后 70% 线性降到 0（t01 ≥ 1 已被存活门移除，此处防御钳 0） */\nexport function flameFadeOutFactor(t01: number, fadeOut = FLAME_FADE_OUT): number {\n  return Math.max(0, flameLerpValue(1, fadeOut, t01));\n}\n\n/** ScaleAcceleration（:3288 num3 × (−1/60)/num）：每 tick² 的收缩加速度 */\nexport function flameScaleAcceleration(scale0: number, ttl = FLAME_TTL): number {\n  return scale0 * (-1 / 60) / ttl;\n}\n\n/** k 次 update 后的 scale（ABasicParticle 递推 scale += Σ accel 的闭式，\n *  供测试锁定二次收缩曲线） */\nexport function flameScaleAfter(scale0: number, accel: number, ticks: number): number {\n  const n = Math.max(0, Math.floor(ticks));\n  return scale0 + accel * (n * (n + 1)) / 2;\n}\n\nexport interface VFlame {\n  active: boolean;\n  /** LocalPosition（世界像素；AnchorPosition 只在原版做 −screenPosition 平移，\n   *  本仓在世界变换内直画 = 世界坐标） */\n  x: number; y: number;\n  /** Velocity（编排器恒 0）——ABasicParticle 通用链保留字段 */\n  vx: number; vy: number;\n  /** Scale（XNA Vector2 双轴，编排器恒同值 → 标量） */\n  scale: number;\n  /** ScaleVelocity（初值 0，逐 tick += ScaleAcceleration） */\n  scaleVel: number;\n  scaleAccel: number;\n  timeSinceSpawn: number;\n  timeToLive: number;\n  fadeOut: number;\n  /** Projectile 贴图号（326/327/328） */\n  tex: number;\n}\n\nconst POOL = 512;\n\nexport class FlameParticles {\n  private pool: VFlame[] = [];\n  private cursor = 0;\n\n  constructor() {\n    for (let i = 0; i < POOL; i++) {\n      this.pool.push({\n        active: false, x: 0, y: 0, vx: 0, vy: 0, scale: 0, scaleVel: 0,\n        scaleAccel: 0, timeSinceSpawn: 0, timeToLive: FLAME_TTL,\n        fadeOut: FLAME_FADE_OUT, tex: 326,\n      });\n    }\n  }\n\n  /** Spawn_WallOfFleshGoatMountFlames 的 FlameParticle 段（:3262-3290）。\n   *  rand 可注入（测试确定性）；返回实际入池束数（满池静默丢弃）。 */\n  spawnGoatFlames(x: number, y: number, rand: () => number = Math.random): number {\n    let n = 0;\n    for (let i = -1; i <= 1; i++) {\n      let slot: VFlame | null = null;\n      for (let k = 0; k < POOL; k++) {\n        const c = this.pool[(this.cursor + k) % POOL];\n        if (!c.active) { slot = c; this.cursor = (this.cursor + k + 1) % POOL; break; }\n      }\n      if (!slot) break;   // 池满：丢弃（VanillaDust 同语义）\n      const num3 = rand() * 0.9 + 0.1;                       // :3268\n      slot.active = true;\n      slot.x = x + i * FLAME_BUNDLE_DX;                      // :3269\n      slot.y = y;\n      slot.vx = 0; slot.vy = 0;                              // :3271 Vector2.Zero\n      slot.scale = num3;                                     // :3290\n      slot.scaleVel = 0;                                     // FetchFromPool 复位（ABasicParticle :34）\n      slot.scaleAccel = flameScaleAcceleration(num3);        // :3288\n      slot.timeSinceSpawn = 0;\n      slot.timeToLive = FLAME_TTL;                           // :3261/:3272\n      slot.fadeOut = FLAME_FADE_OUT;                         // :3287\n      slot.tex = FLAME_TEX[Math.floor(rand() * FLAME_TEX.length) % FLAME_TEX.length];  // :3265\n      n++;\n    }\n    return n;\n  }\n\n  /** ABasicParticle.Update（:39-50）+ FlameParticle.Update（:43-51）：\n   *  速度/位置链保留（编排器恒 0）；scale 二次收缩；到龄移除。 */\n  update(): void {\n    for (const f of this.pool) {\n      if (!f.active) continue;\n      f.x += f.vx; f.y += f.vy;                              // Velocity += 0 后位移\n      f.scaleVel += f.scaleAccel;\n      f.scale += f.scaleVel;\n      f.timeSinceSpawn += 1;\n      if (f.timeSinceSpawn >= f.timeToLive) f.active = false;   // :47-50\n    }\n  }\n\n  /** 活动粒子迭代（绘制用，FIFO 无所谓——同帧内互不遮挡依赖） */\n  *each(): Generator<VFlame> {\n    for (const f of this.pool) if (f.active) yield f;\n  }\n\n  get count(): number {\n    let n = 0;\n    for (const f of this.pool) if (f.active) n++;\n    return n;\n  }\n\n  clear(): void {\n    for (const f of this.pool) f.active = false;\n  }\n}\n\n/** 模块级单例（natureParticles 同款模式：Player 侧 spawn / Renderer 侧 draw） */\nexport const flameParticles = new FlameParticles();\n"

Hmm, careful with bigint conversion of negative/float. Vanilla: `(ulong)LocalPosition.X` — LocalPosition.X is float; cast to ulong (wrap). `(uint)LocalPosition.Y`. In JS bigint: BigInt(Math.trunc(f.x)) then `BigInt.asUintN(64, ...)` for the << 32 shift; and Y as uint32: BigInt.asUintN(32, BigInt(Math.trunc(f.y))). Then XOR with seedBase (which is 48-bit masked in the repo's MASK48 — note vanilla TileFrameSeed is a ulong not masked to 48 bits; the repo's TileFlames model masks to 48 bits; fine, same source as tile flames).

Then the jitter box: `{ s: seed }` and randomInt(box, -2, 3) four times ×2 (x and y interleaved: vanilla draws `new Vector2(RandomInt(-2,3), RandomInt(-2,3))` per copy — one X then one Y per copy, sequential).

Draw per copy:
- pos = (f.x + rx*scale, f.y + ry*scale)
- origin (w/2, h-2)
- ctx.translate(pos); ctx.scale(scale, scale) [if scale !== 1]; drawImage(img, -w/2, -(h-2))

Wait — origin semantics: DrawData(position, ..., origin, scale): the sprite is drawn with its origin at `position`. So dest top-left = position − origin×scale. In canvas: translate(position), scale(scale), drawImage(img, -originX, -originY) with origin = (w/2, h−2).

Two passes:
- Pass ①: globalAlpha = (60/255)×lerp, image = raw img, composite 'source-over'.
- Pass ②: globalAlpha = lerp, image = tinted(tex) (×60/255 multiply tint), composite 'lighter'.

Both at the same transform. So per copy: save/translate/scale, draw ①, set composite lighter + alpha, draw ②, restore.

Total draw calls: 3 particles × 4 copies × 2 passes = 24/frame during goat dash. OK.

Tint cache: key by tex, produce canvas = img tinted ×(60/255) rgb with multiply + destination-in (same as tintedFlameCell). Build lazily per texture (3 entries max), invalidated... textures are static so no invalidation needed.

```
~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/core/Sfx.ts:149: *  playFiles（per-NPC HitSound/DeathSound）、playSfxFile/playUseSound（Item_N 直播）
~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/data/itemUseSound.ts:22:export function itemUseSoundWav(vid: number): string | null {
=== itemUseSound.ts ===
// Item.cs SetDefaults 显式 UseSound 提取（582 件；tools 侧 python 一次性提取，
// 键=物品 id，值=SoundID 名）。Item_N 形可直接映射 public/sounds wav；
// 命名轨（DD2_MonkStaffSwing 等 Trackable）2026-08-13 起经 vanillaSoundFiles
// 解析为变体组 wav（SoundEngine 播放时组内随机取一）。
import sndJson from './vanilla-itemusesound.json';
import { soundTrackName } from './vanillaNpcs';
import soundTracksJson from './vanilla-soundtracks.json';

const TABLE = sndJson as unknown as Record<string, string>;
const SOUND_TRACKS = soundTracksJson as unknown as Record<string, string[]>;

/** LegacySoundStyle 别名常量（SoundID.cs:971/973/:1075）→ wav（Betsy 之 wrath 0.4 音量档
 *  与原型同源，LeafBlower=Item_34） */
const USE_SOUND_ALIASES: Record<string, string[]> = {
  DD2_BetsysWrathShot: ['dd2_betsy_fireball_shot_0', 'dd2_betsy_fireball_shot_1', 'dd2_betsy_fireball_shot_2'],
  DD2_BetsysWrathImpact: ['dd2_betsy_fireball_impact_0', 'dd2_betsy_fireball_impact_1', 'dd2_betsy_fireball_impact_2'],
  LeafBlower: ['Item_34'],
};

/** 物品 → UseSound wav 文件名；无显式赋值/无法解析返回 null。
 *  多变体声轨调用时随机取一（原版 SoundStyle.PlayVariations 语义）。 */
export function itemUseSoundWav(vid: number): string | null {
  const s = vid > 0 ? TABLE[String(vid)] : undefined;
  if (!s) return null;
  if (/^Item_\d+$/.test(s)) return s;
  if (USE_SOUND_ALIASES[s]) {
    const list = USE_SOUND_ALIASES[s];
    return list[Math.floor(Math.random() * list.length)];
  }
  const track = soundTrackName(s);
  const files = track ? SOUND_TRACKS[track] : null;
  if (!files || !files.length) return null;
  return files[Math.floor(Math.random() * files.length)];
}

```

t < 50; t++) fp.update();\n    expect(f.scale).toBeCloseTo(num3 * (1 - 1275 / 3000), 6);   // ≈0.316\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('淡出与存活期（FlameParticle.Update :43-51 / Draw :55）', () => {\n  it('GetLerpValue 反向段（from=1 > to=0.3）：≤0.3 恒 1、≥1 恒 0、间段线性', () => {\n    expect(flameLerpValue(1, 0.3, 0)).toBe(1);\n    expect(flameLerpValue(1, 0.3, 0.3)).toBe(1);\n    expect(flameLerpValue(1, 0.3, 0.65)).toBeCloseTo(0.5);\n    expect(flameLerpValue(1, 0.3, 1)).toBe(0);\n    expect(flameLerpValue(1, 0.3, 1.4)).toBe(0);\n    // 正向段（from<to，Utils.cs:287-297 镜像分支）——函数通用性锁定\n    expect(flameLerpValue(0, 1, -0.5)).toBe(0);\n    expect(flameLerpValue(0, 1, 0.25)).toBeCloseTo(0.25);\n    expect(flameLerpValue(0, 1, 2)).toBe(1);\n  });\n\n  it('flameFadeOutFactor：生命前 30% 全亮、后 70% 线性降到 0', () => {\n    expect(flameFadeOutFactor(0)).toBe(1);\n    expect(flameFadeOutFactor(0.29)).toBe(1);\n    expect(flameFadeOutFactor(0.5)).toBeCloseTo(1 - (0.5 - 0.3) / 0.7);\n    expect(flameFadeOutFactor(0.99)).toBeCloseTo((1 - 0.99) / 0.7, 6);\n  });\n\n  it('存活期 50：第 49 次 update 仍活、第 50 次到龄移除（:47-50 ≥ 即删）', () => {\n    const fp = new FlameParticles();\n    fp.spawnGoatFlames(0, 0, () => 0.5);\n    for (let t = 0; t < 49; t++) fp.update();\n    expect(fp.count).toBe(3);\n    fp.update();\n    expect(fp.count).toBe(0);\n    // 到龄粒子不再被 each 迭代（绘制侧零残留）\n    expect([...fp.each()]).toHaveLength(0);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('池上限（ParticlePool :37-52 无空位语义 → 定长池满丢弃）', () => {\n  it('512 上限：溢出生成被丢、count 不越界、池复用（clear 后可再生成）', () => {\n    const fp = new FlameParticles();\n    const rand = lcg(99);\n    let total = 0, spawned = 0;\n    while (spawned < 4096) {\n      const n = fp.spawnGoatFlames(0, 0, rand);\n      if (n === 0) break;\n      spawned += n;\n      total = fp.count;\n    }\n    expect(total).toBeLessThanOrEqual(512);\n    fp.clear();\n    expect(fp.count).toBe(0);\n    expect(fp.spawnGoatFlames(0, 0, rand)).toBe(3);\n  });\n});\n"
}

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-13T14:25:49.431Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

````
Important find at line 6631: `if (mnt.fullRotation !== 0) ctx.rotate(-mnt.fullRotation);` — this counter-rotation is applied in the diode's local save block, assuming the outer fullRotation was applied as a rotation about the current origin (the texture anchor). With my change, the fullRotation is now applied about a different pivot (the mounted box center). Rotating by −fullRotation about the current origin does NOT exactly undo a rotation about a distant pivot: R_pivot(θ) ∘ R_origin(−θ) = translation, not identity. The diode would be left with a small positional offset (~ pivot distance × θ) and, worse, its orientation would be correct but position shifted by the pivot-origin lever arm.

Let me quantify: origin (anchor) = (p.x + p.w/2 + xOff, p.y + p.h − 29 + yOff + playerOffset) for type 8 (heightBoost 16 → mh=58, mh/2=29). Pivot = (p.x + p.w/2, p.y + p.h − 29). So the pivot differs from the texture anchor by (−xOff, −yOff − playerOffset). xOff = ±1 (xOffset 1), yOff = 4, playerOffset = playerYOffsets[frame] for drill — the drill (8) totalFrames=1 → playerYOffsets likely [0] → playerOffset 0. So delta ≈ (∓1, −4), magnitude ≈ 4.1px. fullRotation max = π/4×0.95/2 ≈ 0.373 rad. Lever offset ≈ |delta| × θ ≈ 4.1 × 0.373 ≈ 1.5px. So the diode/laser would shift ~1.5px from its intended position when canceling naively — before my change the cancellation was exact (both rotations about the same origin).

Correct fix: cancel exactly by applying the inverse transform about the same pivot: translate(pivot)/rotate(−θ)/translate(−pivot) — i.e., reuse applyPlayerMountRotation with angle −fullRotation. Let me do that: replace line 6631 with:

```ts
if (mnt.fullRotation !== 0) {
  const inv = playerMountRotation(mnt.type, -mnt.fullRotation, { x: p.x, y: p.y, w: p.w, h: p.h }, mnt.heightBoost);
  if (inv) applyPlayerMountRotation(ctx, inv);
}
```

Wait — but this block runs inside the transformed space (translate(anchor) + flip + pivot rotation). applyPlayerMountRotation uses absolute world coords — composing inverse about the same world pivot exactly undoes the forward rotation regardless of intermediate transforms? Let's verify: current transform M = T(anchor)·S·T(pivot)·R(θ)·T(−pivot). Applying applyPlayerMountRotation appends T(pivot)·R(−θ)·T(−pivot) at the end: M' = M·T(pivot)·R(−θ)·T(−pivot) = T(anchor)·S·T(pivot)·R(θ)·T(−pivot)·T(pivot)·R(−θ)·T(−pivot) = T(anchor)·S·[T(pivot)·R(θ)·R(−θ)·T(−pivot)] = T(anchor)·S. Exact cancellation ✓.

Also, the comment says "二极管贴图随机身倾斜的原版差异一并近似掉（登记）" — vanilla: the diode texture DOES rotate with the body (only the lasers have ignorePlayerRotation). The repo cancels for both. That's a pre-existing approximation (registered), not mine to change. But my pivot change would perturb the cancellation — so I must fix the cancel to be exact-inverse about the pivot. Let me edit line 6631.
